    label: "At least one Form 1095-A is entered"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8962.h_form_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: f8962.line_a
    printed: "A"
    label: "You cannot take the PTC if your filing status is married filing separately unless you qualify for an exception. See instructions. If you qualify, check the box"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.mfs_exception, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.mfs_three_years, {const: false}]}
    note: "The box is checked by a married-filing-separately filer who qualifies for Exception 2: living apart, unable to file jointly as a victim of domestic abuse or spousal abandonment, and not having used the exception for 3 consecutive years before 2025."
  - id: f8962.h_mfs_bar
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Married filing separately and Exception 2 does not apply (not an applicable taxpayer)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.line_a, {const: false}]}
  - id: f8962.h_low_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Household income is at least 100% of the federal poverty line, or an exception for household income below 100% applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 100, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f8962.estimate_100, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [f8962.line11f, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.alien_below, {const: true}]}
    note: "The exception for estimated household income requires that APTC was paid for the coverage of 1 or more months. This is read as the column (f) annual total (line 11f) being more than zero."
  - id: f8962.h_applicable
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable taxpayer who may take the PTC (lines 7 through 8b and columns (a) through (e) are completed)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_mfs_bar, {const: true}]}}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_low_ok, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: f8962.line7
    printed: "7"
    label: "Applicable figure. Using your line 5 percentage, locate your \"applicable figure\" on the table in the instructions"
    type: Rate
    expr: {op: keyed_lookup, table: ptc_applicable_figure, keys: [f8962.line5]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_applicable
  - id: f8962.line8a
    printed: "8a"
    label: "Annual contribution amount. Multiply line 3 by line 7. Round to nearest whole dollar amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8962.line3, rate: f8962.line7, unit: 100}
    shown_when: f8962.h_applicable
  - id: f8962.line8b
    printed: "8b"
    label: "Monthly contribution amount. Divide line 8a by 12. Round to nearest whole dollar amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: f8962.line8a, rate: {param: ptc.twelfth}, unit: 100}
    shown_when: f8962.h_applicable
  - id: f8962.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Are you allocating policy amounts with another taxpayer or do you want to use the alternative calculation for year of marriage?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.shared_policy, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.divorced_enrolled, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [f8962.shared_policy, f8962.divorced_enrolled, f8962.married_this_year]
    shown_when: f8962.h_any
    note: "Part IV (allocation) and Part V (alternative calculation for year of marriage) are not computed. A return that may need either is refused, so the line computes only as No. Marriage during 2025 with enrollment before the first full month of marriage is refused, because the election cannot be offered."
  - id: f8962.h_nonannual_forms
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Forms 1095-A without full-year coverage and the same column A and column B amount every month"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.a_jan, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_feb, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_mar, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_apr, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_may, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_jun, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_jul, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_aug, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_sep, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_oct, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_nov, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.a_dec, f1095a.a_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_feb, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_mar, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_apr, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_may, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_jun, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_jul, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_aug, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_sep, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_oct, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_nov, f1095a.b_jan]}
              - {op: test, ne: [f1095a.b_dec, f1095a.b_jan]}
    note: "Enrollment for all 12 months is read as column A being more than zero in every month. Column A is tested to be the same in every month by comparing each month with January; for that reason only January is also tested against zero. Column B (the correct SLCSP premium) is compared the same way."
  - id: f8962.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "See the instructions to determine if you can use line 11 or must complete lines 12 through 23. Yes: continue to line 11. No: continue to lines 12-23"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, gt: [f8962.h_form_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_nonannual_forms, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    refuses: [f8962.shared_policy, f8962.divorced_enrolled, f8962.married_this_year]
    shown_when: f8962.h_any
  - id: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 11 columns (a) through (e) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.h_show_annual_f
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 11 column (f) is completed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_any, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 12 through 23 columns (a) through (e) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.h_show_monthly_f
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Lines 12 through 23 column (f) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_any, {const: true}]}
  - id: f8962.line11a
    printed: "11(a)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.line33a, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
  - id: f8962.line11b
    printed: "11(b)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: f1095a
      field: f1095a.line33b
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
    note: "Column B is entered from only one form for policies in the same state, and added across states and across separate plans before a December marriage. This reads f1095a.b_used as whether a form's column B is counted under those rules (the answer given in f1095a.b_counted). The catalog does not state this definition."
  - id: f8962.line11c
    printed: "11(c)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual contribution amount (line 8a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8a}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
  - id: f8962.line11d
    printed: "11(d)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line11b, take: f8962.line11c}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
  - id: f8962.h_line11e_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 11 column (a) or column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line11a, f8962.line11d]}
  - id: f8962.line11e
    printed: "11(e)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line11e_calc}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_ae
    note: "A taxpayer who is not an applicable taxpayer cannot take the PTC: married filing separately without Exception 2, or household income below 100% without an exception. Such a taxpayer completes only column (f), and line 24 is -0-. Column (e) is therefore zero for them."
  - id: f8962.line11f
    printed: "11(f)"
    label: "Annual Totals: Annual advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, line 33C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.line33c, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_annual_f

  # ---- January (line 12) ----
  - id: f8962.line12a
    printed: "12(a)"
    label: "January: Monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_jan, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.line12b
    printed: "12(b)"
    label: "January: Monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_jan, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.h_show_12
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "January columns (c) through (e) are completed (column (a) or (b) not blank)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_show_monthly_ae, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [f8962.line12a, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [f8962.line12b, {const: 0}]}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line12c
    printed: "12(c)"
    label: "January: Monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8b}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_12
  - id: f8962.line12d
    printed: "12(d)"
    label: "January: Monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line12b, take: f8962.line12c}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_12
  - id: f8962.h_line12e_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "January: smaller of column (a) or column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line12a, f8962.line12d]}
  - id: f8962.line12e
    printed: "12(e)"
    label: "January: Monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line12e_calc}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_12
  - id: f8962.line12f
    printed: "12(f)"
    label: "January: Monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_jan, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_f

  # ---- February (line 13) ----
  - id: f8962.line13a
    printed: "13(a)"
    label: "February: Monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_feb, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.line13b
    printed: "13(b)"
    label: "February: Monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_feb, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.h_show_13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "February columns (c) through (e) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_show_monthly_ae, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [f8962.line13a, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [f8962.line13b, {const: 0}]}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line13c
    printed: "13(c)"
    label: "February: Monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8b}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_13
  - id: f8962.line13d
    printed: "13(d)"
    label: "February: Monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line13b, take: f8962.line13c}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_13
  - id: f8962.h_line13e_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "February: smaller of column (a) or column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line13a, f8962.line13d]}
  - id: f8962.line13e
    printed: "13(e)"
    label: "February: Monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line13e_calc}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_13
  - id: f8962.line13f
    printed: "13(f)"
    label: "February: Monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_feb, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_f

  # ---- March (line 14) ----
  - id: f8962.line14a
    printed: "14(a)"
    label: "March: Monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_mar, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.line14b
    printed: "14(b)"
    label: "March: Monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_mar, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.h_show_14
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "March columns (c) through (e) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_show_monthly_ae, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [f8962.line14a, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [f8962.line14b, {const: 0}]}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line14c
    printed: "14(c)"
    label: "March: Monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8b}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_14
  - id: f8962.line14d
    printed: "14(d)"
    label: "March: Monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line14b, take: f8962.line14c}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_14
  - id: f8962.h_line14e_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "March: smaller of column (a) or column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line14a, f8962.line14d]}
  - id: f8962.line14e
    printed: "14(e)"
    label: "March: Monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line14e_calc}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_14
  - id: f8962.line14f
    printed: "14(f)"
    label: "March: Monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_mar, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_f

  # ---- April (line 15) ----
  - id: f8962.line15a
    printed: "15(a)"
    label: "April: Monthly enrollment premiums (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.a_apr, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.line15b
    printed: "15(b)"
    label: "April: Monthly applicable SLCSP premium (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column B)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.b_apr, where: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}, {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_used, {const: true}]}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_ae
  - id: f8962.h_show_15
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "April columns (c) through (e) are completed"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_show_monthly_ae, {const: true}]}, {op: test, any: [{op: test, gt: [f8962.line15a, {const: 0}]}, {op: test, gt: [f8962.line15b, {const: 0}]}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line15c
    printed: "15(c)"
    label: "April: Monthly contribution amount (amount from line 8b)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f8962.line8b}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_15
  - id: f8962.line15d
    printed: "15(d)"
    label: "April: Monthly maximum premium assistance (subtract (c) from (b); if zero or less, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line15b, take: f8962.line15c}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_15
  - id: f8962.h_line15e_calc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "April: smaller of column (a) or column (d)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line15a, f8962.line15d]}
  - id: f8962.line15e
    printed: "15(e)"
    label: "April: Monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line15e_calc}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_15

    label: "December: Monthly PTC allowed (smaller of (a) or (d))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: choose, alternatives: [{guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.h_line23e_calc}, {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.h_applicable, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_zero}]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_23
  - id: f8962.line23f
    printed: "23(f)"
    label: "December: Monthly advance payment of PTC (Form(s) 1095-A, lines 21-32, column C)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f1095a, field: f1095a.in_c_dec, where: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.void, {const: false}]}}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show_monthly_f

  # ---- Totals ----
  - id: f8962.h_mec_refuse
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Someone on the return was eligible for, or enrolled in, other minimum essential coverage in a month of Marketplace coverage"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.other_coverage, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.mec_job_enrolled, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.other_coverage, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.job_offer, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.mec_job_affordable, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.other_coverage, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.mec_government, {const: true}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.other_coverage, {const: true}]}, {op: test, eq: [f8962.mec_other, {const: true}]}]}
  - id: f8962.h_sum_e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 through 23, column (e)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.line12e, f8962.line13e, f8962.line14e, f8962.line15e, f8962.line16e, f8962.line17e, f8962.line18e, f8962.line19e, f8962.line20e, f8962.line21e, f8962.line22e, f8962.line23e]}
  - id: f8962.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Total PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (e), or add lines 12 through 23, column (e), and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.line11e}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_sum_e}
    refuses: [f8962.h_mec_refuse, f8962.hra, f8962.not_lawful, f8962.incarcerated, f8962.medicaid_reversed, f8962.unpaid, f8962.midmonth_start, f8962.premium_refunded, f8962.same_state_counted, f8962.shared_policy, f8962.divorced_enrolled, f8962.married_this_year]
    shown_when: f8962.h_any
    note: "The following change the coverage family, the monthly amounts or column (e), and are not computed, so they are refused: eligibility for other minimum essential coverage; a QSEHRA or individual coverage HRA; anyone not lawfully present or incarcerated; a reversed Medicaid determination; unpaid premiums; coverage starting mid-month; refunded premiums; and overlapping same-state column B amounts. Coverage starting on the day of a birth, adoption, placement or court order is not counted as mid-month."
  - id: f8962.h_sum_f
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Add lines 12 through 23, column (f)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f8962.line12f, f8962.line13f, f8962.line14f, f8962.line15f, f8962.line16f, f8962.line17f, f8962.line18f, f8962.line19f, f8962.line20f, f8962.line21f, f8962.line22f, f8962.line23f]}
  - id: f8962.line25
    printed: "25"
    label: "Advance payment of PTC. Enter the amount from line 11, column (f), or add lines 12 through 23, column (f), and enter the total here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: true}]}, then: f8962.line11f}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f8962.line10, {const: false}]}, then: f8962.h_sum_f}
    refuses: [f8962.no_family, f8962.not_lawful, f8962.shared_policy, f8962.divorced_enrolled]
    shown_when: f8962.h_any
  - id: f8962.h_show26
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 24 is equal to or greater than line 25 (line 26 is completed)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_any, {const: true}]}, {op: test, ge: [f8962.line24, f8962.line25]}]}
  - id: f8962.line26
    printed: "26"
    label: "Net PTC. If line 24 is greater than line 25, subtract line 25 from line 24. Enter the difference here and on Schedule 3 (Form 1040), line 9. If line 24 equals line 25, enter -0-. Stop here. If line 25 is greater than line 24, leave this line blank and continue to line 27"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line24, take: f8962.line25}
    shown_when: f8962.h_show26
  - id: f8962.h_excess
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 25 is greater than line 24 (Part III is completed)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [f8962.line25, f8962.line24]}
  - id: f8962.line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Excess advance payment of PTC. If line 25 is greater than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 25. Enter the difference here"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f8962.line25, take: f8962.line24}
    shown_when: f8962.h_excess
  - id: f8962.h_repay_200
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5 repayment limitation, line 5 less than 200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_200}
  - id: f8962.h_repay_300
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5 repayment limitation, line 5 at least 200 but less than 300"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_300}
  - id: f8962.h_repay_400
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Table 5 repayment limitation, line 5 at least 300 but less than 400"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ptc.repay_400}
  - id: f8962.h_show28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 28 is completed (Part III applies and line 5 is less than 400)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [f8962.h_excess, {const: true}]}, {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}]}
  - id: f8962.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Repayment limitation (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 200, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_200}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 200, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 300, type: Count}]}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_300}
        - {guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 300, type: Count}]}, {op: test, lt: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}]}, then: f8962.h_repay_400}
        - {guard: {op: test, ge: [f8962.line5, {const: 400, type: Count}]}, then: f8962.line27}
    refuses: [f8962.not_lawful]
    shown_when: f8962.h_show28
    note: "Table 5 gives one column for Single and one for any other filing status; the parameters are keyed by filing status. If line 5 is 400 or more, line 28 is left blank and line 29 is the full line 27. For that case line 28 holds the line 27 amount, so the smaller of lines 27 and 28 is line 27, and the line is not shown. The supplied text says that a self-employed filer claiming the self-employed health insurance deduction must take the line 28 amount from Pub. 974. No screening input covers this, so it is not encoded."
  - id: f8962.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Excess advance PTC repayment. Enter the smaller of line 27 or line 28 here and on Schedule 2 (Form 1040), line 1a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [f8962.line27, f8962.line28]}
    shown_when: f8962.h_excess
```

Second transcription's note: The following change the coverage family, the monthly amounts or column (e), and are not computed, so they are refused: eligibility for other minimum essential coverage; a QSEHRA or individual coverage HRA; anyone not lawfully present or incarcerated; a reversed Medicaid determination; unpaid premiums; coverage starting mid-month; refunded premiums; and overlapping same-state column B amounts. Coverage starting on the day of a birth, adoption, placement or court order is not counted as mid-month.
